        Idea:
Vendor hermes-agent into the megaplan repo as a subpackage called `agent` using git subtree (preserving history). Job A scope: (1) map reachable-module surface from megaplan's hermes_worker integration; (2) git-subtree vendor /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent/ at megaplan/agent/ preserving history; (3) rewire megaplan/hermes_worker.py to import from in-tree megaplan.agent package; (4) remove check_hermes_available() gating; (5) delete obvious dead weight (evals/, landingpage/, website/, demo/, assets/, node_modules/, package.json, package-lock.json, tinker-atropos/, honcho_integration/, mini-swe-agent/, gateway/, cron/, hermes_cli/, cli.py, auto_improve/, batch_runner.py, rl_cli.py, datagen-config-examples/, root *.json benchmark artifacts, RELEASE_v*.md, setup-hermes.sh, cli-config.yaml.example); (6) verify megaplan still imports and hermes_worker resolves. Out of scope (Job B later): skills/, tools/ filtering, acp_adapter, acp_registry, environments/.

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Vendor hermes-agent as `megaplan/agent/` subpackage (Job A)

## Overview
Vendor `hermes-agent` into `megaplan` at `megaplan/agent/` via `git subtree` (history preserved). Integrate via a `sys.path` prepend shim in `megaplan/agent/__init__.py` so hermes's original top-level imports resolve without rewriting any vendored file.

**Iter-6 refinement: DRY the shim+error-conversion via one helper.** Prior iterations tracked execution sites by name and kept missing one (iter-5 said three; the repo has four: `megaplan/hermes_worker.py:290 run_hermes_step`, `megaplan/review/parallel.py:80 _run_check`, `megaplan/review/parallel.py:209 _run_criteria_verdict`, `megaplan/parallel_critique.py:51 _run_check`). Fix: introduce one helper `_import_hermes_runtime()` in `megaplan/hermes_worker.py` that (a) triggers the shim, (b) imports `AIAgent` and `SessionDB`, (c) converts `ImportError` into `CliError("agent_deps_missing", ...)`. All four execution sites call the helper. The AST gate becomes a positive structural check: "zero `from run_agent import` / `from hermes_state import` statements anywhere in `megaplan/**/*.py` outside `megaplan/agent/` AND outside `_import_hermes_runtime` itself." A future 5th site that forgets the helper is caught by the gate; a correct 5th site that uses the helper passes without modification.

**Settled decisions (locked; not revisited):**
- `vendoring-strategy-shim`: sys.path shim in `megaplan/agent/__init__.py`.
- `shim-trigger-placement-rule`: shim activated only through `_import_hermes_runtime()`; availability helpers use filesystem checks.
- `availability-helpers-filesystem-check`: `_is_agent_available("hermes")` and `detect_available_agents()` use `(Path(__file__).resolve()... / "agent" / "run_agent.py").is_file()`.
- `hermes-cli-retained`: keep vendored in Job A.
- `agent-optional-extra`: `[project.optional-dependencies] agent = [...]`.
- `key-pool-sibling-fallback-removed`: drop `../hermes-agent/auto_improve/api_keys.json`.
- `preserve-sys-modules-test-fakes`: keep `sys.modules["run_agent"]/["hermes_state"]` test fakes unchanged.
- `callers-agent-deps-missing-try-except`: every hermes import site routes through a single error-converting helper.
- `subtree-no-squash`.
- `job-b-scope-fences`: `skills/`, `tools/`, `acp_adapter*`, `acp_registry*`, `environments/`, `hermes_cli/` stay.
- `hermetic-validation-probe`: `HOME=$(mktemp -d)` + `import model_tools`.
- `ast-gate-positive-structural-check`: iter-6 — positive check ("no direct hermes imports except in the helper") replaces any hardcoded function-name whitelist.

**Deviations from the brief (unchanged):**
1. Keep `megaplan/agent/hermes_cli/` — reachable modules import it at load time.
2. Fix two sibling-repo references: `megaplan/key_pool.py:62-74`, `megaplan/workers.py:1662-1665`.

## Main Phase

### Step 1: Pre-flight — clean tree + snapshot hermes HEAD (`git`)
**Scope:** Small
1. Stash/commit dirty files (`megaplan/execute/core.py`, `megaplan/prompts/execute_doc.py`, `megaplan/prompts/planning.py`).
2. Record `git -C /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent rev-parse HEAD` and default branch; cite the SHA in the merge commit body.

### Step 2: Subtree-merge hermes-agent at `megaplan/agent/` (`git subtree`)
**Scope:** Small
1. `git subtree add --prefix=megaplan/agent /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent main` — no `--squash`.
2. Confirm `run_agent.py`, `hermes_state.py`, `hermes_cli/`, `model_tools.py`, `pyproject.toml` present.

### Step 3: Diagnostic — enumerate vendored names + audit `gateway`/`cron`/`honcho_integration` references
**Scope:** Small
1. `grep -hrE "^(from|import) [a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_]*" megaplan/agent/ | awk '{print $2}' | cut -d. -f1 | sort -u` — record top-level names.
2. `grep -rE 'import_module\("([a-z_]+)\.' megaplan/agent/ | sort -u` — record dynamic-import first-segments.
3. `grep -rnE "gateway|cron|honcho_integration" megaplan/agent/ --include='*.py' | grep -vE "^megaplan/agent/(gateway|cron|honcho_integration)/"` — for each match, inspect context. Each reference MUST be wrapped in `try: ... except ImportError:` OR unreachable in Job A config. If any unguarded top-level/import-time reference exists, retain that directory in Step 7 with rationale (same pattern as `hermes_cli/`).
4. **Final execution-site enumeration (prevents iter-5 undercount):** `grep -rnE "^\s*from (run_agent|hermes_state) import" megaplan/ --include='*.py' | grep -v "^megaplan/agent/"` — output MUST match the four known sites (`hermes_worker.py:290-291`, `review/parallel.py:80-81`, `review/parallel.py:209-210`, `parallel_critique.py:51-52`). If any new site appears post-subtree (e.g. in tests or other files), add it to Step 5's caller list before proceeding.
5. Diagnostic only — no files modified.

### Step 4: Install the sys.path shim (`megaplan/agent/__init__.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. Write `megaplan/agent/__init__.py`:
   ```python
   """Vendored hermes-agent subpackage.

   Importing this module prepends its directory to sys.path so hermes's
   original top-level imports resolve without rewriting vendored code.
   Activated via `megaplan.hermes_worker._import_hermes_runtime()` only.
   """
   import os as _os
   import sys as _sys

   _agent_dir = _os.path.dirname(__file__)
   if _agent_dir not in _sys.path:
       _sys.path.insert(0, _agent_dir)
   ```
2. No other file under `megaplan/agent/` is modified.

### Step 5: Rewire megaplan — single helper for shim+import+error-conversion, filesystem availability checks, `agent_deps_missing` UX, supporting infrastructure (`megaplan/*.py`)
**Scope:** Medium

1. **Add `_import_hermes_runtime()` helper to `megaplan/hermes_worker.py`** (near the other module-private helpers, above `run_hermes_step`):
   ```python
   def _import_hermes_runtime():
       """Trigger the sys.path shim and return (AIAgent, SessionDB).

       All hermes execution sites route through this helper so that the
       `[agent]`-extras-missing case produces a uniform actionable error
       message. Do NOT import `run_agent` / `hermes_state` directly from
       megaplan code outside this function — the AST gate in Step 9.1
       enforces this.
       """
       import megaplan.agent  # noqa: F401 — sys.path shim
       try:
           from run_agent import AIAgent
           from hermes_state import SessionDB
       except ImportError as exc:
           from megaplan.types import CliError
           raise CliError(
               "agent_deps_missing",
               "hermes backend requires: pip install 'megaplan-harness[agent]'",
           ) from exc
       return AIAgent, SessionDB
   ```
2. **Replace direct hermes imports with helper calls at all FOUR execution sites:**
   - **`megaplan/hermes_worker.py:290-291`** in `run_hermes_step`: replace `from run_agent import AIAgent` / `from hermes_state import SessionDB` with `AIAgent, SessionDB = _import_hermes_runtime()`.
   - **`megaplan/review/parallel.py:80-81`** in `_run_check`: same pattern — `from megaplan.hermes_worker import _import_hermes_runtime` at function top (inside the function body), then `AIAgent, SessionDB = _import_hermes_runtime()`.
   - **`megaplan/review/parallel.py:209-210`** in `_run_criteria_verdict`: same pattern as above.
   - **`megaplan/parallel_critique.py:51-52`** in `_run_check`: same pattern.
3. **`megaplan/workers.py:1576-1584` — `_is_agent_available` via filesystem (no imports):**
   ```python
   def _is_agent_available(agent: str) -> bool:
       if agent == "hermes":
           from pathlib import Path
           return (Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "agent" / "run_agent.py").is_file()
       return bool(shutil.which(agent))
   ```
4. **`megaplan/_core/io.py:263-274` — `detect_available_agents` via filesystem (no imports):**
   ```python
   def detect_available_agents() -> list[str]:
       import megaplan._core as _core_pkg
       _shutil_ref = _core_pkg.shutil
       available = [a for a in KNOWN_AGENTS if a != "hermes" and _shutil_ref.which(a)]
       from pathlib import Path
       if (Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "agent" / "run_agent.py").is_file():
           available.append("hermes")
       return available
   ```
5. **Delete `check_hermes_available()`** from `megaplan/hermes_worker.py:23-49` along with its `from hermes_cli.config import get_env_path` usage.
6. **Rewrite gating in `megaplan/workers.py:1643-1658`** — replace both `check_hermes_available()` call sites with inline branches that raise `CliError("agent_deps_missing", ...)` when explicit hermes selection meets `not _is_agent_available("hermes")` (same code as iter 5 — keeps the actionable error for the case where vendored files are genuinely absent, e.g. git-clone-without-subtree).
7. **Supporting infrastructure:**
   - `megaplan/key_pool.py:62-74` — drop the `repo_root.parent / "hermes-agent" / ...` sibling-repo fallback.
   - `megaplan/workers.py:1662-1665` — change text to `"Install claude or codex, or pip install 'megaplan-harness[agent]' for hermes."`.

### Step 6: Tests — no changes required
**Scope:** Trivial
1. `tests/test_parallel_critique.py:404-407` and `tests/test_parallel_review.py:198-201, 281-284` keep their `monkeypatch.setitem(sys.modules, "run_agent", ...)` / `sys.modules["hermes_state"]` fake pattern. The helper does `from run_agent import AIAgent; from hermes_state import SessionDB` — Python resolves `sys.modules` before sys.path, so the fakes intercept and the helper returns (FakeAIAgent, FakeSessionDB).
2. Confirmation: run `pytest tests/test_parallel_critique.py tests/test_parallel_review.py -q` after Step 5 and verify green.

**Scope:** Small

**Scope:** Medium

### Step 9: Verify (`bash`, `pytest`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Cheap grep gates:**
   - `grep -r "check_hermes_available" megaplan/ tests/` → zero.
   - `grep -n "hermes-agent" megaplan/key_pool.py` → zero.
   - **Positive AST gate (addresses all_locations, all_locations-1, correctness-3 by construction):** script that parses every `megaplan/**/*.py` outside `megaplan/agent/` with `ast` and asserts: (a) zero module-top `Import`/`ImportFrom` targets `megaplan.agent`; (b) zero `ImportFrom` statements with `module in {"run_agent", "hermes_state"}` anywhere EXCEPT inside the function `_import_hermes_runtime` in `megaplan/hermes_worker.py`; (c) `_import_hermes_runtime` body contains a `Try` whose body has `ImportFrom(module="run_agent")` and `ImportFrom(module="hermes_state")` and whose `except ImportError` handler raises a `Call` to `CliError` with first arg literal `"agent_deps_missing"`. Emit pass/fail with the offending file:line for any violation.
2. **Strengthened isolation probe:**
   ```
   python -c "
   import sys
   before = list(sys.path)
   import megaplan
   from megaplan.workers import _is_agent_available
   from megaplan._core.io import detect_available_agents
   _ = _is_agent_available('claude')
   _ = _is_agent_available('codex')
   _ = _is_agent_available('hermes')
   _ = detect_available_agents()
   after = list(sys.path)
   assert before == after, f'sys.path mutated: before={before}, after={after}'
   print('OK')
   "
   ```
   Must exit 0 without hermes runtime deps installed.
3. **Bare-Python megaplan import probe** (no hermes deps):
   ```
   python -c "import megaplan.hermes_worker, megaplan.workers, megaplan.review.parallel, megaplan.parallel_critique, megaplan._core.io, megaplan.key_pool"
   ```
4. **Hermetic shim-coverage probe** (venv with `pip install -e '.[agent]'`):
   ```
   HOME=$(mktemp -d) python -c "
   from megaplan.hermes_worker import _import_hermes_runtime
   AIAgent, SessionDB = _import_hermes_runtime()
   import model_tools
   print('OK')
   "
   ```
   Exercises the helper (which triggers the shim) + dynamic `tools.*` loader via `model_tools` module-scope imports.
5. **`agent_deps_missing` end-to-end for ALL four execution paths** (venv WITHOUT `[agent]`):
   ```
   python -c "
   from megaplan.hermes_worker import _import_hermes_runtime
   try:
       _import_hermes_runtime()
       raise SystemExit('expected CliError')
   except Exception as exc:
       assert exc.__class__.__name__ == 'CliError' and 'agent_deps_missing' in str(exc.code), exc
       print('OK: helper raises agent_deps_missing')
   "
   ```
   Since all four execution sites call the helper, verifying the helper covers all four paths. Additionally, smoke-test one real CLI path: `megaplan --agent hermes <args>` must produce `agent_deps_missing`.
6. **Targeted pytest:** `pytest tests/test_parallel_critique.py tests/test_parallel_review.py -q`.
7. **Full suite:** `pytest tests/ -q`.
8. **CLI smoke + isolation:** `megaplan --help` exit 0; re-run probe 9.2 afterward.

## Execution Order
1. Clean tree + snapshot hermes HEAD (Step 1).
2. Subtree merge (Step 2).
3. Diagnostic enumeration + gateway/cron/honcho audit + execution-site final count (Step 3).
4. Install sys.path shim (Step 4).
5. Add `_import_hermes_runtime()` helper + route all four sites through it + filesystem availability checks + delete `check_hermes_available` + `agent_deps_missing` CliError branch in `workers.resolve_agent_mode` + key_pool/workers text fixes (Step 5).
6. Confirm tests pass unchanged (Step 6).
7. Delete dead weight (Step 7), honoring Step 3.3 retentions.
8. Packaging + README (Step 8).
9. Validate (Step 9).

## Validation Order
1. Cheap greps + positive AST gate (proves by construction that no direct hermes import exists outside the helper).
2. Strengthened isolation probe (sys.path unchanged after availability calls).
3. Bare-Python megaplan import probe.
4. Hermetic helper+dynamic-loader probe under `HOME=$(mktemp -d)`.
5. `agent_deps_missing` probe on the helper (covers all four sites since all route through it) + one CLI smoke.
6. Targeted pytest.
7. Full pytest.
8. `megaplan --help` + re-verify isolation.
9. (info) Manual hermes-backed phase run after `[agent]` install.

The `agent_deps_missing` UX is delivered via: (a) `_import_hermes_runtime()`'s try/except at every real execution site, AND (b) `workers.resolve_agent_mode`'s inline branch for the case where `_is_agent_available(\"hermes\")` returns False (vendored files genuinely absent, e.g. subtree skipped). Two complementary layers.",
    "No test changes. `sys.modules[\"run_agent\"]`/`sys.modules[\"hermes_state\"]` fakes intercept the helper's imports because Python resolves sys.modules before sys.path \u2014 the helper returns (FakeAIAgent, FakeSessionDB) to callers.",
    "Step 9.1's AST gate is a POSITIVE STRUCTURAL check (no hardcoded function-name whitelist). Any future 5th execution site that uses the helper passes; one that imports hermes directly fails fast. Makes the gate self-correcting as megaplan evolves.",
    "Helper re-uses existing `CliError` from `megaplan.types`; no new exception class.",
    "`megaplan/key_pool.py` drops sibling-repo fallback; `~/.hermes/.env` flow unchanged.",
    "Hermes runtime deps ship as `[project.optional-dependencies] agent = [...]`, harvested verbatim from `megaplan/agent/pyproject.toml`."
  ],

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Vendor hermes-agent as `megaplan/agent/` subpackage (Job A)

## Overview
Vendor `hermes-agent` into `megaplan` at `megaplan/agent/` via `git subtree` (history preserved). Integrate via a `sys.path` prepend shim in `megaplan/agent/__init__.py` so hermes's original top-level imports resolve without rewriting any vendored file.

**Iter-6 refinement: DRY the shim+error-conversion via one helper.** Prior iterations tracked execution sites by name and kept missing one (iter-5 said three; the repo has four: `megaplan/hermes_worker.py:290 run_hermes_step`, `megaplan/review/parallel.py:80 _run_check`, `megaplan/review/parallel.py:209 _run_criteria_verdict`, `megaplan/parallel_critique.py:51 _run_check`). Fix: introduce one helper `_import_hermes_runtime()` in `megaplan/hermes_worker.py` that (a) triggers the shim, (b) imports `AIAgent` and `SessionDB`, (c) converts `ImportError` into `CliError("agent_deps_missing", ...)`. All four execution sites call the helper. The AST gate becomes a positive structural check: "zero `from run_agent import` / `from hermes_state import` statements anywhere in `megaplan/**/*.py` outside `megaplan/agent/` AND outside `_import_hermes_runtime` itself." A future 5th site that forgets the helper is caught by the gate; a correct 5th site that uses the helper passes without modification.

**Settled decisions (locked; not revisited):**
- `vendoring-strategy-shim`: sys.path shim in `megaplan/agent/__init__.py`.
- `shim-trigger-placement-rule`: shim activated only through `_import_hermes_runtime()`; availability helpers use filesystem checks.
- `availability-helpers-filesystem-check`: `_is_agent_available("hermes")` and `detect_available_agents()` use `(Path(__file__).resolve()... / "agent" / "run_agent.py").is_file()`.
- `hermes-cli-retained`: keep vendored in Job A.
- `agent-optional-extra`: `[project.optional-dependencies] agent = [...]`.
- `key-pool-sibling-fallback-removed`: drop `../hermes-agent/auto_improve/api_keys.json`.
- `preserve-sys-modules-test-fakes`: keep `sys.modules["run_agent"]/["hermes_state"]` test fakes unchanged.
- `callers-agent-deps-missing-try-except`: every hermes import site routes through a single error-converting helper.
- `subtree-no-squash`.
- `job-b-scope-fences`: `skills/`, `tools/`, `acp_adapter*`, `acp_registry*`, `environments/`, `hermes_cli/` stay.
- `hermetic-validation-probe`: `HOME=$(mktemp -d)` + `import model_tools`.
- `ast-gate-positive-structural-check`: iter-6 — positive check ("no direct hermes imports except in the helper") replaces any hardcoded function-name whitelist.

**Deviations from the brief (unchanged):**

### Step 4: Install the sys.path shim (`megaplan/agent/__init__.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. Write `megaplan/agent/__init__.py`:
   ```python
   """Vendored hermes-agent subpackage.

   Importing this module prepends its directory to sys.path so hermes's
   original top-level imports resolve without rewriting vendored code.
   Activated via `megaplan.hermes_worker._import_hermes_runtime()` only.
   """
   import os as _os
   import sys as _sys

   _agent_dir = _os.path.dirname(__file__)
   if _agent_dir not in _sys.path:
       _sys.path.insert(0, _agent_dir)
   ```
2. No other file under `megaplan/agent/` is modified.

### Step 5: Rewire megaplan — single helper for shim+import+error-conversion, filesystem availability checks, `agent_deps_missing` UX, supporting infrastructure (`megaplan/*.py`)
**Scope:** Medium

1. **Add `_import_hermes_runtime()` helper to `megaplan/hermes_worker.py`** (near the other module-private helpers, above `run_hermes_step`):
   ```python
   def _import_hermes_runtime():
       """Trigger the sys.path shim and return (AIAgent, SessionDB).

       All hermes execution sites route through this helper so that the
       `[agent]`-extras-missing case produces a uniform actionable error
       message. Do NOT import `run_agent` / `hermes_state` directly from
       megaplan code outside this function — the AST gate in Step 9.1
       enforces this.
       """
       import megaplan.agent  # noqa: F401 — sys.path shim
       try:
           from run_agent import AIAgent
           from hermes_state import SessionDB
       except ImportError as exc:
           from megaplan.types import CliError
           raise CliError(
               "agent_deps_missing",
               "hermes backend requires: pip install 'megaplan-harness[agent]'",
           ) from exc
       return AIAgent, SessionDB
   ```
2. **Replace direct hermes imports with helper calls at all FOUR execution sites:**
   - **`megaplan/hermes_worker.py:290-291`** in `run_hermes_step`: replace `from run_agent import AIAgent` / `from hermes_state import SessionDB` with `AIAgent, SessionDB = _import_hermes_runtime()`.
   - **`megaplan/review/parallel.py:80-81`** in `_run_check`: same pattern — `from megaplan.hermes_worker import _import_hermes_runtime` at function top (inside the function body), then `AIAgent, SessionDB = _import_hermes_runtime()`.
   - **`megaplan/review/parallel.py:209-210`** in `_run_criteria_verdict`: same pattern as above.
   - **`megaplan/parallel_critique.py:51-52`** in `_run_check`: same pattern.
3. **`megaplan/workers.py:1576-1584` — `_is_agent_available` via filesystem (no imports):**
   ```python
   def _is_agent_available(agent: str) -> bool:
       if agent == "hermes":
           from pathlib import Path
           return (Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "agent" / "run_agent.py").is_file()
       return bool(shutil.which(agent))
   ```
4. **`megaplan/_core/io.py:263-274` — `detect_available_agents` via filesystem (no imports):**
   ```python
   def detect_available_agents() -> list[str]:
       import megaplan._core as _core_pkg
       _shutil_ref = _core_pkg.shutil
       available = [a for a in KNOWN_AGENTS if a != "hermes" and _shutil_ref.which(a)]
       from pathlib import Path
       if (Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "agent" / "run_agent.py").is_file():
           available.append("hermes")
       return available
   ```
5. **Delete `check_hermes_available()`** from `megaplan/hermes_worker.py:23-49` along with its `from hermes_cli.config import get_env_path` usage.
6. **Rewrite gating in `megaplan/workers.py:1643-1658`** — replace both `check_hermes_available()` call sites with inline branches that raise `CliError("agent_deps_missing", ...)` when explicit hermes selection meets `not _is_agent_available("hermes")` (same code as iter 5 — keeps the actionable error for the case where vendored files are genuinely absent, e.g. git-clone-without-subtree).

The helper does `from run_agent import AIAgent; from hermes_state import SessionDB` — Python resolves `sys.modules` before sys.path, so the fakes intercept and the helper returns (FakeAIAgent, FakeSessionDB).

   ```
   python -c "
   import sys
   before = list(sys.path)
   import megaplan
   from megaplan.workers import _is_agent_available
   from megaplan._core.io import detect_available_agents
   _ = _is_agent_available('claude')
   _ = _is_agent_available('codex')
   _ = _is_agent_available('hermes')
   _ = detect_available_agents()
   after = list(sys.path)
   assert before == after, f'sys.path mutated: before={before}, after={after}'
   print('OK')
   "
   ```
   Must exit 0 without hermes runtime deps installed.
3. **Bare-Python megaplan import probe** (no hermes deps):
   ```
   python -c "import megaplan.hermes_worker, megaplan.workers, megaplan.review.parallel, megaplan.parallel_critique, megaplan._core.io, megaplan.key_pool"
   ```
4. **Hermetic shim-coverage probe** (venv with `pip install -e '.[agent]'`):
   ```
   HOME=$(mktemp -d) python -c "
   from megaplan.hermes_worker import _import_hermes_runtime
   AIAgent, SessionDB = _import_hermes_runtime()
   import model_tools
   print('OK')
   "
   ```
   Exercises the helper (which triggers the shim) + dynamic `tools.*` loader via `model_tools` module-scope imports.
5. **`agent_deps_missing` end-to-end for ALL four execution paths** (venv WITHOUT `[agent]`):
   ```
   python -c "
   from megaplan.hermes_worker import _import_hermes_runtime
   try:
       _import_hermes_runtime()
       raise SystemExit('expected CliError')
   except Exception as exc:
       assert exc.__class__.__name__ == 'CliError' and 'agent_deps_missing' in str(exc.code), exc
       print('OK: helper raises agent_deps_missing')
   "
   ```
   Since all four execution sites call the helper, verifying the helper covers all four paths. Additionally, smoke-test one real CLI path: `megaplan --agent hermes <args>` must produce `agent_deps_missing`.
6. **Targeted pytest:** `pytest tests/test_parallel_critique.py tests/test_parallel_review.py -q`.
7. **Full suite:** `pytest tests/ -q`.
8. **CLI smoke + isolation:** `megaplan --help` exit 0; re-run probe 9.2 afterward.

## Execution Order
1. Clean tree + snapshot hermes HEAD (Step 1).
2. Subtree merge (Step 2).
3. Diagnostic enumeration + gateway/cron/honcho audit + execution-site final count (Step 3).
4. Install sys.path shim (Step 4).
5. Add `_import_hermes_runtime()` helper + route all four sites through it + filesystem availability checks + delete `check_hermes_available` + `agent_deps_missing` CliError branch in `workers.resolve_agent_mode` + key_pool/workers text fixes (Step 5).
6. Confirm tests pass unchanged (Step 6).
7. Delete dead weight (Step 7), honoring Step 3.3 retentions.
8. Packaging + README (Step 8).
9. Validate (Step 9).

## Validation Order
1. Cheap greps + positive AST gate (proves by construction that no direct hermes import exists outside the helper).
2. Strengthened isolation probe (sys.path unchanged after availability calls).
3. Bare-Python megaplan import probe.
4. Hermetic helper+dynamic-loader probe under `HOME=$(mktemp -d)`.
5. `agent_deps_missing` probe on the helper (covers all four sites since all route through it) + one CLI smoke.
6. Targeted pytest.
7. Full pytest.
8. `megaplan --help` + re-verify isolation.
9. (info) Manual hermes-backed phase run after `[agent]` install.

    {
      "id": "DEBT-011",
      "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: a new critical issue is introduced on mobile. step 4.9 says to remove entrypage's mobile sticky bottom nav and 'use the same `footercontent` slot in submissioncarouselcard', but `src/components/submissioncarouselcard.tsx:217-221` renders `footercontent` inside `classname=\"hidden md:block\"`. in the current repo, the mobile sticky nav at `src/pages/entrypage.tsx:283-340` is the only mobile surface for back/next and the score toggle, so the revised plan would remove working mobile controls and replace them with a slot that is explicitly hidden on phones.",

      ]

    {
      "id": "DEBT-047",
      "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: issue hints: the plan now wires `mode=auto|chain|idle`, but the user-requested `auto.idea_file` and `chain.spec` still have no deployment path into the container. reigh's readme uploads the idea file explicitly before running `megaplan init`, and `chain.sh` exits if `/workspace/chain.yaml` is missing, so a plan that only sets these paths in `cloud.yaml` without also staging the files still leaves the requested runner modes incomplete.",

      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: if the implementation really wants `footercontent` to carry mobile actions, the plan must also include a supporting change in `src/components/submissioncarouselcard.tsx` or render a separate mobile control surface outside the card. right now step 4.9 removes the mobile nav but does not list `submissioncarouselcard.tsx:217-221` as a place that must change, so the required glue for mobile controls is missing.",

      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the prior missing locations in `megaplan/workers.py` and `tests/test_workers.py` are now covered, but one supporting-infrastructure path is still absent from the steps: the repo-root generated schema copy under `.megaplan/schemas/review.json` and the tests that read it directly. without either explicitly regenerating that file or changing those tests to materialize schemas in a temp root, the plan can still leave the runtime-schema copy out of sync with the raw registry.",

    {
      "id": "DEBT-035",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the plan adds _set_active_step and _clear_active_step helpers to handlers.py. _run_worker (handlers.py:135) is modified to accept a `resolved` parameter. but _run_worker is also called directly by handle_critique's sequential fallback at line 801 and 803. these calls currently don't pass `resolved`. the plan's step 2.6 for handle_critique says to set active_step after resolution at line 794 \u2014 but the _run_worker calls at 801/803 would re-resolve the agent internally (since `resolved` isn't passed). this means the agent is resolved twice on the critique sequential path. the double-resolution is wasteful but correct (same result). however, to match the plan's goal of 'resolve_agent_mode() is called once per handler invocation', the resolved tuple should be passed to these _run_worker calls too.",

    {
      "id": "DEBT-014",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: i checked the skip caller path against the real hook behavior and `removefromqueue(entryid)` looks redundant. `scoring.skipentry(entryid)` optimistically adds the id to `skippedentryids` in `src/hooks/usescoring.ts:245-249`, and `usecarouselqueue` already reactively filters skipped entries from `submissions[]` in `src/hooks/usecarouselqueue.ts:154-165`. calling `removefromqueue()` immediately afterward is safe, but it duplicates an existing state transition and should be justified as an explicit ux optimization rather than required logic.",

      "id": "DEBT-015",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: the direct ui sign-in caller is still important here. `scorepanel` invokes its own `onrequiresignin` prop when an unauthenticated user interacts with the score ui (`src/components/scorepanel.tsx:499-515`), which is independent of the scoring hook's internal `requireauth()` path. the plan should be read as requiring that caller to dispatch `open_sign_in_modal`; otherwise one caller remains broken even if `submitscore()` is correctly wired.",

      {
        "id": "DEBT-009",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the mobile action-surface plan is technically incorrect for the current component structure. `submissioncarouselcard` hides `footercontent` below the `md` breakpoint (`src/components/submissioncarouselcard.tsx:219`), while `submissionscarouselpage` also hides the score panel on mobile via `asidecontent={<div classname=\"hidden md:block\">...` and passes `hideactions` to `scorepanel` at `src/pages/submissionscarouselpage.tsx:250-263,347`. reusing that pattern for entrypage would leave mobile users with no navigation controls and no scoring controls.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "move-the-entry-page-into-20260401-0450"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-010",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: i checked the sign-in callback path again. `submissionslayout` already wires `usescoring(..., { onrequiresignin: () => pagemachine.dispatch({ type: 'open_sign_in_modal' }) })` at `src/pages/submissionslayout.tsx:38-40`, and `scorepanel` still has its own `onrequiresignin` prop. the revised step 4.2 is acceptable only if it explicitly rewires that prop to `pagemachine.dispatch`; otherwise removing local modal state would orphan the direct ui callback. the plan hints at this, but it remains easy to misread because the scoring-context callback and the scorepanel prop are separate mechanisms.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "move-the-entry-page-into-20260401-0450"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-029",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the new diff-helper proposal against the repo's existing execution-evidence semantics. `validate_execution_evidence()` in `megaplan/evaluation.py:109-175` uses `git status --short`, so untracked files count as real changed files today, but step 3 proposes `collect_git_diff_patch(project_dir)` via `git diff head`; by git behavior that will not include untracked files, so heavy review could miss exactly the newly created files that execute and review already treat as part of the patch.",

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Vendor hermes-agent as `megaplan/agent/` subpackage (Job A)

## Overview
Vendor `hermes-agent` into `megaplan` at `megaplan/agent/` via `git subtree` (history preserved). Integrate via a `sys.path` prepend shim in `megaplan/agent/__init__.py` so hermes's original top-level imports resolve without rewriting any vendored file.

**Iter-6 refinement: DRY the shim+error-conversion via one helper.** Prior iterations tracked execution sites by name and kept missing one (iter-5 said three; the repo has four: `megaplan/hermes_worker.py:290 run_hermes_step`, `megaplan/review/parallel.py:80 _run_check`, `megaplan/review/parallel.py:209 _run_criteria_verdict`, `megaplan/parallel_critique.py:51 _run_check`). Fix: introduce one helper `_import_hermes_runtime()` in `megaplan/hermes_worker.py` that (a) triggers the shim, (b) imports `AIAgent` and `SessionDB`, (c) converts `ImportError` into `CliError("agent_deps_missing", ...)`. All four execution sites call the helper. The AST gate becomes a positive structural check: "zero `from run_agent import` / `from hermes_state import` statements anywhere in `megaplan/**/*.py` outside `megaplan/agent/` AND outside `_import_hermes_runtime` itself." A future 5th site that forgets the helper is caught by the gate; a correct 5th site that uses the helper passes without modification.

**Settled decisions (locked; not revisited):**
- `vendoring-strategy-shim`: sys.path shim in `megaplan/agent/__init__.py`.
- `shim-trigger-placement-rule`: shim activated only through `_import_hermes_runtime()`; availability helpers use filesystem checks.
- `availability-helpers-filesystem-check`: `_is_agent_available("hermes")` and `detect_available_agents()` use `(Path(__file__).resolve()... / "agent" / "run_agent.py").is_file()`.
- `hermes-cli-retained`: keep vendored in Job A.
- `agent-optional-extra`: `[project.optional-dependencies] agent = [...]`.
- `key-pool-sibling-fallback-removed`: drop `../hermes-agent/auto_improve/api_keys.json`.
- `preserve-sys-modules-test-fakes`: keep `sys.modules["run_agent"]/["hermes_state"]` test fakes unchanged.
- `callers-agent-deps-missing-try-except`: every hermes import site routes through a single error-converting helper.
- `subtree-no-squash`.
- `job-b-scope-fences`: `skills/`, `tools/`, `acp_adapter*`, `acp_registry*`, `environments/`, `hermes_cli/` stay.
- `hermetic-validation-probe`: `HOME=$(mktemp -d)` + `import model_tools`.
- `ast-gate-positive-structural-check`: iter-6 — positive check ("no direct hermes imports except in the helper") replaces any hardcoded function-name whitelist.

**Deviations from the brief (unchanged):**
1. Keep `megaplan/agent/hermes_cli/` — reachable modules import it at load time.
2. Fix two sibling-repo references: `megaplan/key_pool.py:62-74`, `megaplan/workers.py:1662-1665`.

**Scope:** Small

### Step 4: Install the sys.path shim (`megaplan/agent/__init__.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. Write `megaplan/agent/__init__.py`:
   ```python
   """Vendored hermes-agent subpackage.

   Importing this module prepends its directory to sys.path so hermes's
   original top-level imports resolve without rewriting vendored code.
   Activated via `megaplan.hermes_worker._import_hermes_runtime()` only.
   """
   import os as _os
   import sys as _sys

   _agent_dir = _os.path.dirname(__file__)
   if _agent_dir not in _sys.path:
       _sys.path.insert(0, _agent_dir)
   ```
2. No other file under `megaplan/agent/` is modified.

### Step 5: Rewire megaplan — single helper for shim+import+error-conversion, filesystem availability checks, `agent_deps_missing` UX, supporting infrastructure (`megaplan/*.py`)
**Scope:** Medium

1. **Add `_import_hermes_runtime()` helper to `megaplan/hermes_worker.py`** (near the other module-private helpers, above `run_hermes_step`):
   ```python
   def _import_hermes_runtime():
       """Trigger the sys.path shim and return (AIAgent, SessionDB).

       All hermes execution sites route through this helper so that the
       `[agent]`-extras-missing case produces a uniform actionable error
       message. Do NOT import `run_agent` / `hermes_state` directly from
       megaplan code outside this function — the AST gate in Step 9.1
       enforces this.
       """
       import megaplan.agent  # noqa: F401 — sys.path shim
       try:
           from run_agent import AIAgent
           from hermes_state import SessionDB
       except ImportError as exc:
           from megaplan.types import CliError
           raise CliError(
               "agent_deps_missing",
               "hermes backend requires: pip install 'megaplan-harness[agent]'",
           ) from exc
       return AIAgent, SessionDB
   ```
2. **Replace direct hermes imports with helper calls at all FOUR execution sites:**
   - **`megaplan/hermes_worker.py:290-291`** in `run_hermes_step`: replace `from run_agent import AIAgent` / `from hermes_state import SessionDB` with `AIAgent, SessionDB = _import_hermes_runtime()`.
   - **`megaplan/review/parallel.py:80-81`** in `_run_check`: same pattern — `from megaplan.hermes_worker import _import_hermes_runtime` at function top (inside the function body), then `AIAgent, SessionDB = _import_hermes_runtime()`.
   - **`megaplan/review/parallel.py:209-210`** in `_run_criteria_verdict`: same pattern as above.
   - **`megaplan/parallel_critique.py:51-52`** in `_run_check`: same pattern.
3. **`megaplan/workers.py:1576-1584` — `_is_agent_available` via filesystem (no imports):**
   ```python
   def _is_agent_available(agent: str) -> bool:
       if agent == "hermes":
           from pathlib import Path
           return (Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "agent" / "run_agent.py").is_file()
       return bool(shutil.which(agent))
   ```
4. **`megaplan/_core/io.py:263-274` — `detect_available_agents` via filesystem (no imports):**
   ```python
   def detect_available_agents() -> list[str]:
       import megaplan._core as _core_pkg
       _shutil_ref = _core_pkg.shutil
       available = [a for a in KNOWN_AGENTS if a != "hermes" and _shutil_ref.which(a)]
       from pathlib import Path
       if (Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "agent" / "run_agent.py").is_file():
           available.append("hermes")
       return available
   ```
5. **Delete `check_hermes_available()`** from `megaplan/hermes_worker.py:23-49` along with its `from hermes_cli.config import get_env_path` usage.
6. **Rewrite gating in `megaplan/workers.py:1643-1658`** — replace both `check_hermes_available()` call sites with inline branches that raise `CliError("agent_deps_missing", ...)` when explicit hermes selection meets `not _is_agent_available("hermes")` (same code as iter 5 — keeps the actionable error for the case where vendored files are genuinely absent, e.g. git-clone-without-subtree).
7. **Supporting infrastructure:**
   - `megaplan/key_pool.py:62-74` — drop the `repo_root.parent / "hermes-agent" / ...` sibling-repo fallback.
   - `megaplan/workers.py:1662-1665` — change text to `"Install claude or codex, or pip install 'megaplan-harness[agent]' for hermes."`.

### Step 6: Tests — no changes required
**Scope:** Trivial
1. `tests/test_parallel_critique.py:404-407` and `tests/test_parallel_review.py:198-201, 281-284` keep their `monkeypatch.setitem(sys.modules, "run_agent", ...)` / `sys.modules["hermes_state"]` fake pattern. The helper does `from run_agent import AIAgent; from hermes_state import SessionDB` — Python resolves `sys.modules` before sys.path, so the fakes intercept and the helper returns (FakeAIAgent, FakeSessionDB).
2. Confirmation: run `pytest tests/test_parallel_critique.py tests/test_parallel_review.py -q` after Step 5 and verify green.

### Step 7: Delete dead weight (`git rm` inside `megaplan/agent/`)
**Scope:** Small
1. `git rm -r` brief's list minus `hermes_cli/` and any Step 3.3 retentions:
   ```
   evals/ landingpage/ website/ demo/ assets/ node_modules/
   package.json package-lock.json
   tinker-atropos/ mini-swe-agent/
   cli.py auto_improve/ batch_runner.py rl_cli.py
   datagen-config-examples/
   RELEASE_v*.md setup-hermes.sh cli-config.yaml.example
   ```
   For `gateway/`, `cron/`, `honcho_integration/`: delete ONLY if Step 3.3 confirms references are try/except-guarded or unreachable.
2. Root-level `*.json` benchmark artifacts: `git ls-files 'megaplan/agent/*.json'`, delete obvious benchmark outputs only.
3. Document retentions (if any) in commit body.

### Step 8: Packaging — `[agent]` extra (`pyproject.toml`, `README.md`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. Harvest runtime deps verbatim from `megaplan/agent/pyproject.toml`.
2. Add `[project.optional-dependencies] agent = [...]` to `megaplan/pyproject.toml`. Baseline `dependencies` stays `["PyYAML>=6.0"]`.
3. Confirm `[tool.hatch.build.targets.wheel]` includes `megaplan/agent/`. Exclude the nested `megaplan/agent/pyproject.toml` from the wheel if hatch complains.
4. README line 23 → `pip install 'megaplan-harness[agent]'`; one-liner about the `[agent]` extra.

### Step 9: Verify (`bash`, `pytest`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Cheap grep gates:**
   - `grep -r "check_hermes_available" megaplan/ tests/` → zero.
   - `grep -n "hermes-agent" megaplan/key_pool.py` → zero.
   - **Positive AST gate (addresses all_locations, all_locations-1, correctness-3 by construction):** script that parses every `megaplan/**/*.py` outside `megaplan/agent/` with `ast` and asserts: (a) zero module-top `Import`/`ImportFrom` targets `megaplan.agent`; (b) zero `ImportFrom` statements with `module in {"run_agent", "hermes_state"}` anywhere EXCEPT inside the function `_import_hermes_runtime` in `megaplan/hermes_worker.py`; (c) `_import_hermes_runtime` body contains a `Try` whose body has `ImportFrom(module="run_agent")` and `ImportFrom(module="hermes_state")` and whose `except ImportError` handler raises a `Call` to `CliError` with first arg literal `"agent_deps_missing"`. Emit pass/fail with the offending file:line for any violation.
2. **Strengthened isolation probe:**
   ```
   python -c "
   import sys
   before = list(sys.path)
   import megaplan
   from megaplan.workers import _is_agent_available
   from megaplan._core.io import detect_available_agents
   _ = _is_agent_available('claude')
   _ = _is_agent_available('codex')
   _ = _is_agent_available('hermes')
   _ = detect_available_agents()
   after = list(sys.path)
   assert before == after, f'sys.path mutated: before={before}, after={after}'
   print('OK')
   "
   ```
   Must exit 0 without hermes runtime deps installed.
3. **Bare-Python megaplan import probe** (no hermes deps):
   ```
   python -c "import megaplan.hermes_worker, megaplan.workers, megaplan.review.parallel, megaplan.parallel_critique, megaplan._core.io, megaplan.key_pool"
   ```
4. **Hermetic shim-coverage probe** (venv with `pip install -e '.[agent]'`):
   ```
   HOME=$(mktemp -d) python -c "
   from megaplan.hermes_worker import _import_hermes_runtime
   AIAgent, SessionDB = _import_hermes_runtime()
   import model_tools
   print('OK')
   "
   ```
   Exercises the helper (which triggers the shim) + dynamic `tools.*` loader via `model_tools` module-scope imports.
5. **`agent_deps_missing` end-to-end for ALL four execution paths** (venv WITHOUT `[agent]`):
   ```
   python -c "
   from megaplan.hermes_worker import _import_hermes_runtime
   try:
       _import_hermes_runtime()
       raise SystemExit('expected CliError')
   except Exception as exc:
       assert exc.__class__.__name__ == 'CliError' and 'agent_deps_missing' in str(exc.code), exc
       print('OK: helper raises agent_deps_missing')
   "
   ```
   Since all four execution sites call the helper, verifying the helper covers all four paths. Additionally, smoke-test one real CLI path: `megaplan --agent hermes <args>` must produce `agent_deps_missing`.
6. **Targeted pytest:** `pytest tests/test_parallel_critique.py tests/test_parallel_review.py -q`.
7. **Full suite:** `pytest tests/ -q`.
8. **CLI smoke + isolation:** `megaplan --help` exit 0; re-run probe 9.2 afterward.

## Execution Order
1. Clean tree + snapshot hermes HEAD (Step 1).
2. Subtree merge (Step 2).
3. Diagnostic enumeration + gateway/cron/honcho audit + execution-site final count (Step 3).
4. Install sys.path shim (Step 4).
5. Add `_import_hermes_runtime()` helper + route all four sites through it + filesystem availability checks + delete `check_hermes_available` + `agent_deps_missing` CliError branch in `workers.resolve_agent_mode` + key_pool/workers text fixes (Step 5).
6. Confirm tests pass unchanged (Step 6).
7. Delete dead weight (Step 7), honoring Step 3.3 retentions.
8. Packaging + README (Step 8).
9. Validate (Step 9).

## Validation Order
1. Cheap greps + positive AST gate (proves by construction that no direct hermes import exists outside the helper).
2. Strengthened isolation probe (sys.path unchanged after availability calls).
3. Bare-Python megaplan import probe.
4. Hermetic helper+dynamic-loader probe under `HOME=$(mktemp -d)`.
5. `agent_deps_missing` probe on the helper (covers all four sites since all route through it) + one CLI smoke.
6. Targeted pytest.
7. Full pytest.
8. `megaplan --help` + re-verify isolation.
9. (info) Manual hermes-backed phase run after `[agent]` install.

    {
      "id": "DEBT-009",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the mobile action-surface plan is technically incorrect for the current component structure. `submissioncarouselcard` hides `footercontent` below the `md` breakpoint (`src/components/submissioncarouselcard.tsx:219`), while `submissionscarouselpage` also hides the score panel on mobile via `asidecontent={<div classname=\"hidden md:block\">...` and passes `hideactions` to `scorepanel` at `src/pages/submissionscarouselpage.tsx:250-263,347`. reusing that pattern for entrypage would leave mobile users with no navigation controls and no scoring controls.",

      ]

When in doubt, flag it — the gate can accept tradeoffs, but it can't act on findings it never sees.

Good: {"detail": "Checked callers of nthroot_mod in solveset.py line 1205 — passes prime moduli only, consistent with the fix.", "flagged": false}
Good: {"detail": "The fix handles empty tuples but not single-element tuples which need a trailing comma.", "flagged": true}
Bad: {"detail": "No issue found", "flagged": false}  ← too brief, will be rejected
Bad: {"detail": "The hints suggest approach X but the plan uses Y. However Y is consistent with X's intent.", "flagged": false}  ← a different approach than the hints IS a flag. You found a divergence — flag it. The gate decides if it's acceptable.

        # Implementation Plan: Vendor hermes-agent as `megaplan/agent/` subpackage (Job A)

## Overview
Vendor `hermes-agent` into `megaplan` at `megaplan/agent/` via `git subtree` (history preserved). Integrate via a `sys.path` prepend shim in `megaplan/agent/__init__.py` so hermes's original top-level imports resolve without rewriting any vendored file.

**Iter-6 refinement: DRY the shim+error-conversion via one helper.** Prior iterations tracked execution sites by name and kept missing one (iter-5 said three; the repo has four: `megaplan/hermes_worker.py:290 run_hermes_step`, `megaplan/review/parallel.py:80 _run_check`, `megaplan/review/parallel.py:209 _run_criteria_verdict`, `megaplan/parallel_critique.py:51 _run_check`). Fix: introduce one helper `_import_hermes_runtime()` in `megaplan/hermes_worker.py` that (a) triggers the shim, (b) imports `AIAgent` and `SessionDB`, (c) converts `ImportError` into `CliError("agent_deps_missing", ...)`. All four execution sites call the helper. The AST gate becomes a positive structural check: "zero `from run_agent import` / `from hermes_state import` statements anywhere in `megaplan/**/*.py` outside `megaplan/agent/` AND outside `_import_hermes_runtime` itself." A future 5th site that forgets the helper is caught by the gate; a correct 5th site that uses the helper passes without modification.

**Settled decisions (locked; not revisited):**
- `vendoring-strategy-shim`: sys.path shim in `megaplan/agent/__init__.py`.
- `shim-trigger-placement-rule`: shim activated only through `_import_hermes_runtime()`; availability helpers use filesystem checks.
- `availability-helpers-filesystem-check`: `_is_agent_available("hermes")` and `detect_available_agents()` use `(Path(__file__).resolve()... / "agent" / "run_agent.py").is_file()`.
- `hermes-cli-retained`: keep vendored in Job A.
- `agent-optional-extra`: `[project.optional-dependencies] agent = [...]`.
- `key-pool-sibling-fallback-removed`: drop `../hermes-agent/auto_improve/api_keys.json`.
- `preserve-sys-modules-test-fakes`: keep `sys.modules["run_agent"]/["hermes_state"]` test fakes unchanged.
- `callers-agent-deps-missing-try-except`: every hermes import site routes through a single error-converting helper.
- `subtree-no-squash`.
- `job-b-scope-fences`: `skills/`, `tools/`, `acp_adapter*`, `acp_registry*`, `environments/`, `hermes_cli/` stay.
- `hermetic-validation-probe`: `HOME=$(mktemp -d)` + `import model_tools`.
- `ast-gate-positive-structural-check`: iter-6 — positive check ("no direct hermes imports except in the helper") replaces any hardcoded function-name whitelist.

**Deviations from the brief (unchanged):**
1. Keep `megaplan/agent/hermes_cli/` — reachable modules import it at load time.
2. Fix two sibling-repo references: `megaplan/key_pool.py:62-74`, `megaplan/workers.py:1662-1665`.

## Main Phase

### Step 1: Pre-flight — clean tree + snapshot hermes HEAD (`git`)
**Scope:** Small
1. Stash/commit dirty files (`megaplan/execute/core.py`, `megaplan/prompts/execute_doc.py`, `megaplan/prompts/planning.py`).
2. Record `git -C /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent rev-parse HEAD` and default branch; cite the SHA in the merge commit body.

### Step 2: Subtree-merge hermes-agent at `megaplan/agent/` (`git subtree`)
**Scope:** Small
1. `git subtree add --prefix=megaplan/agent /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent main` — no `--squash`.
2. Confirm `run_agent.py`, `hermes_state.py`, `hermes_cli/`, `model_tools.py`, `pyproject.toml` present.

### Step 3: Diagnostic — enumerate vendored names + audit `gateway`/`cron`/`honcho_integration` references
**Scope:** Small
1. `grep -hrE "^(from|import) [a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_]*" megaplan/agent/ | awk '{print $2}' | cut -d. -f1 | sort -u` — record top-level names.
2. `grep -rE 'import_module\("([a-z_]+)\.' megaplan/agent/ | sort -u` — record dynamic-import first-segments.
3. `grep -rnE "gateway|cron|honcho_integration" megaplan/agent/ --include='*.py' | grep -vE "^megaplan/agent/(gateway|cron|honcho_integration)/"` — for each match, inspect context. Each reference MUST be wrapped in `try: ... except ImportError:` OR unreachable in Job A config. If any unguarded top-level/import-time reference exists, retain that directory in Step 7 with rationale (same pattern as `hermes_cli/`).
4. **Final execution-site enumeration (prevents iter-5 undercount):** `grep -rnE "^\s*from (run_agent|hermes_state) import" megaplan/ --include='*.py' | grep -v "^megaplan/agent/"` — output MUST match the four known sites (`hermes_worker.py:290-291`, `review/parallel.py:80-81`, `review/parallel.py:209-210`, `parallel_critique.py:51-52`). If any new site appears post-subtree (e.g. in tests or other files), add it to Step 5's caller list before proceeding.
5. Diagnostic only — no files modified.

### Step 4: Install the sys.path shim (`megaplan/agent/__init__.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. Write `megaplan/agent/__init__.py`:
   ```python
   """Vendored hermes-agent subpackage.

   Importing this module prepends its directory to sys.path so hermes's
   original top-level imports resolve without rewriting vendored code.
   Activated via `megaplan.hermes_worker._import_hermes_runtime()` only.
   """
   import os as _os
   import sys as _sys

   _agent_dir = _os.path.dirname(__file__)
   if _agent_dir not in _sys.path:
       _sys.path.insert(0, _agent_dir)
   ```
2. No other file under `megaplan/agent/` is modified.

### Step 5: Rewire megaplan — single helper for shim+import+error-conversion, filesystem availability checks, `agent_deps_missing` UX, supporting infrastructure (`megaplan/*.py`)
**Scope:** Medium

1. **Add `_import_hermes_runtime()` helper to `megaplan/hermes_worker.py`** (near the other module-private helpers, above `run_hermes_step`):
   ```python
   def _import_hermes_runtime():
       """Trigger the sys.path shim and return (AIAgent, SessionDB).

       All hermes execution sites route through this helper so that the
       `[agent]`-extras-missing case produces a uniform actionable error
       message. Do NOT import `run_agent` / `hermes_state` directly from
       megaplan code outside this function — the AST gate in Step 9.1
       enforces this.
       """
       import megaplan.agent  # noqa: F401 — sys.path shim
       try:
           from run_agent import AIAgent
           from hermes_state import SessionDB
       except ImportError as exc:
           from megaplan.types import CliError
           raise CliError(
               "agent_deps_missing",
               "hermes backend requires: pip install 'megaplan-harness[agent]'",
           ) from exc
       return AIAgent, SessionDB
   ```
2. **Replace direct hermes imports with helper calls at all FOUR execution sites:**
   - **`megaplan/hermes_worker.py:290-291`** in `run_hermes_step`: replace `from run_agent import AIAgent` / `from hermes_state import SessionDB` with `AIAgent, SessionDB = _import_hermes_runtime()`.
   - **`megaplan/review/parallel.py:80-81`** in `_run_check`: same pattern — `from megaplan.hermes_worker import _import_hermes_runtime` at function top (inside the function body), then `AIAgent, SessionDB = _import_hermes_runtime()`.
   - **`megaplan/review/parallel.py:209-210`** in `_run_criteria_verdict`: same pattern as above.
   - **`megaplan/parallel_critique.py:51-52`** in `_run_check`: same pattern.
3. **`megaplan/workers.py:1576-1584` — `_is_agent_available` via filesystem (no imports):**
   ```python
   def _is_agent_available(agent: str) -> bool:
       if agent == "hermes":
           from pathlib import Path
           return (Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "agent" / "run_agent.py").is_file()
       return bool(shutil.which(agent))
   ```
4. **`megaplan/_core/io.py:263-274` — `detect_available_agents` via filesystem (no imports):**
   ```python
   def detect_available_agents() -> list[str]:
       import megaplan._core as _core_pkg
       _shutil_ref = _core_pkg.shutil
       available = [a for a in KNOWN_AGENTS if a != "hermes" and _shutil_ref.which(a)]
       from pathlib import Path
       if (Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "agent" / "run_agent.py").is_file():
           available.append("hermes")
       return available
   ```
5. **Delete `check_hermes_available()`** from `megaplan/hermes_worker.py:23-49` along with its `from hermes_cli.config import get_env_path` usage.
6. **Rewrite gating in `megaplan/workers.py:1643-1658`** — replace both `check_hermes_available()` call sites with inline branches that raise `CliError("agent_deps_missing", ...)` when explicit hermes selection meets `not _is_agent_available("hermes")` (same code as iter 5 — keeps the actionable error for the case where vendored files are genuinely absent, e.g. git-clone-without-subtree).
7. **Supporting infrastructure:**
   - `megaplan/key_pool.py:62-74` — drop the `repo_root.parent / "hermes-agent" / ...` sibling-repo fallback.
   - `megaplan/workers.py:1662-1665` — change text to `"Install claude or codex, or pip install 'megaplan-harness[agent]' for hermes."`.

**Scope:** Trivial

The helper does `from run_agent import AIAgent; from hermes_state import SessionDB` — Python resolves `sys.modules` before sys.path, so the fakes intercept and the helper returns (FakeAIAgent, FakeSessionDB).

### Step 9: Verify (`bash`, `pytest`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Cheap grep gates:**
   - `grep -r "check_hermes_available" megaplan/ tests/` → zero.
   - `grep -n "hermes-agent" megaplan/key_pool.py` → zero.
   - **Positive AST gate (addresses all_locations, all_locations-1, correctness-3 by construction):** script that parses every `megaplan/**/*.py` outside `megaplan/agent/` with `ast` and asserts: (a) zero module-top `Import`/`ImportFrom` targets `megaplan.agent`; (b) zero `ImportFrom` statements with `module in {"run_agent", "hermes_state"}` anywhere EXCEPT inside the function `_import_hermes_runtime` in `megaplan/hermes_worker.py`; (c) `_import_hermes_runtime` body contains a `Try` whose body has `ImportFrom(module="run_agent")` and `ImportFrom(module="hermes_state")` and whose `except ImportError` handler raises a `Call` to `CliError` with first arg literal `"agent_deps_missing"`. Emit pass/fail with the offending file:line for any violation.
2. **Strengthened isolation probe:**
   ```
   python -c "
   import sys
   before = list(sys.path)
   import megaplan
   from megaplan.workers import _is_agent_available
   from megaplan._core.io import detect_available_agents
   _ = _is_agent_available('claude')
   _ = _is_agent_available('codex')
   _ = _is_agent_available('hermes')
   _ = detect_available_agents()
   after = list(sys.path)
   assert before == after, f'sys.path mutated: before={before}, after={after}'
   print('OK')
   "
   ```
   Must exit 0 without hermes runtime deps installed.
3. **Bare-Python megaplan import probe** (no hermes deps):
   ```
   python -c "import megaplan.hermes_worker, megaplan.workers, megaplan.review.parallel, megaplan.parallel_critique, megaplan._core.io, megaplan.key_pool"
   ```
4. **Hermetic shim-coverage probe** (venv with `pip install -e '.[agent]'`):
   ```
   HOME=$(mktemp -d) python -c "
   from megaplan.hermes_worker import _import_hermes_runtime
   AIAgent, SessionDB = _import_hermes_runtime()
   import model_tools
   print('OK')
   "
   ```
   Exercises the helper (which triggers the shim) + dynamic `tools.*` loader via `model_tools` module-scope imports.
5. **`agent_deps_missing` end-to-end for ALL four execution paths** (venv WITHOUT `[agent]`):
   ```
   python -c "
   from megaplan.hermes_worker import _import_hermes_runtime
   try:
       _import_hermes_runtime()
       raise SystemExit('expected CliError')
   except Exception as exc:
       assert exc.__class__.__name__ == 'CliError' and 'agent_deps_missing' in str(exc.code), exc
       print('OK: helper raises agent_deps_missing')
   "
   ```
   Since all four execution sites call the helper, verifying the helper covers all four paths. Additionally, smoke-test one real CLI path: `megaplan --agent hermes <args>` must produce `agent_deps_missing`.
6. **Targeted pytest:** `pytest tests/test_parallel_critique.py tests/test_parallel_review.py -q`.
7. **Full suite:** `pytest tests/ -q`.
8. **CLI smoke + isolation:** `megaplan --help` exit 0; re-run probe 9.2 afterward.

## Execution Order
1. Clean tree + snapshot hermes HEAD (Step 1).
2. Subtree merge (Step 2).
3. Diagnostic enumeration + gateway/cron/honcho audit + execution-site final count (Step 3).
4. Install sys.path shim (Step 4).
5. Add `_import_hermes_runtime()` helper + route all four sites through it + filesystem availability checks + delete `check_hermes_available` + `agent_deps_missing` CliError branch in `workers.resolve_agent_mode` + key_pool/workers text fixes (Step 5).
6. Confirm tests pass unchanged (Step 6).
7. Delete dead weight (Step 7), honoring Step 3.3 retentions.
8. Packaging + README (Step 8).
9. Validate (Step 9).

## Validation Order
1. Cheap greps + positive AST gate (proves by construction that no direct hermes import exists outside the helper).
2. Strengthened isolation probe (sys.path unchanged after availability calls).
3. Bare-Python megaplan import probe.
4. Hermetic helper+dynamic-loader probe under `HOME=$(mktemp -d)`.
5. `agent_deps_missing` probe on the helper (covers all four sites since all route through it) + one CLI smoke.
6. Targeted pytest.
7. Full pytest.
8. `megaplan --help` + re-verify isolation.
9. (info) Manual hermes-backed phase run after `[agent]` install.

        "id": "DEBT-009",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the mobile action-surface plan is technically incorrect for the current component structure. `submissioncarouselcard` hides `footercontent` below the `md` breakpoint (`src/components/submissioncarouselcard.tsx:219`), while `submissionscarouselpage` also hides the score panel on mobile via `asidecontent={<div classname=\"hidden md:block\">...` and passes `hideactions` to `scorepanel` at `src/pages/submissionscarouselpage.tsx:250-263,347`. reusing that pattern for entrypage would leave mobile users with no navigation controls and no scoring controls.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,

        ]
      },

        "id": "DEBT-010",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: i checked the sign-in callback path again. `submissionslayout` already wires `usescoring(..., { onrequiresignin: () => pagemachine.dispatch({ type: 'open_sign_in_modal' }) })` at `src/pages/submissionslayout.tsx:38-40`, and `scorepanel` still has its own `onrequiresignin` prop. the revised step 4.2 is acceptable only if it explicitly rewires that prop to `pagemachine.dispatch`; otherwise removing local modal state would orphan the direct ui callback. the plan hints at this, but it remains easy to misread because the scoring-context callback and the scorepanel prop are separate mechanisms.",
